Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The math_logic operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 635. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 854 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to math_logic structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 635/854 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
